I used to text my brother a lot after my shifts, but... I don't know. Now I just let his messages sit there. I don't really feel the pull to answer him. It's... yeah. It's the same with people at work, really. I used to joke around with the regulars, but lately I just... keep my face straight. I just say what I have to say, and that's it.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any significant changes in your appetite or sleep patterns?\n\nPatient: I guess... my eating's been kind of all over the place. Um... some weeks I barely touch the staff meal at work. Other nights... I don't know, I get home and just eat whatever's in the kitchen. Just to... feel settled, I guess. Sleep is... yeah, it's about the same.\n\nDoctor: Have you had a period of at least two weeks where you felt better, with less of these empty and useless feelings, since you started feeling this way about a year and eight months ago?\n\nPatient: Yeah, um... I guess there was a stretch. Maybe a few weeks where... I wasn't feeling quite as down. Getting through my shifts was a bit easier. \n\nBut... I don't know. It's not like everything was fine. I was still... I still heard the whispering at the condo. And I still had that feeling of being watched... especially when I was looking over my mortgage paperwork. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about the whispering you mentioned earlier, what did it sound like?\n\nPatient: It's... just a voice. Um... usually when it's quiet. Like if I'm closing up the restaurant, or... just sitting by myself at the condo. \n\nIt's low. Sometimes it just says my name. Other times it... it whispers that I'm going to lose everything. It sounds real. I usually... I look around to see if someone's there. But there isn't.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing the whispering voice?\n\nPatient: I don't know... a while now. I don't think so. Just... just the voice and the feeling. They're just... always kind of there.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any other unusual perceptions or thoughts that seem real to you, besides the whispering voice and feeling of being watched?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. Just... just the voice and the feeling. Nothing else really.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the whispering voice is real, or do you think it's just your mind playing a trick on you?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know. It feels real. But... I don't really know.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe the whispering voice is telling the truth when it says you're going to lose everything or that you're useless?\n\nPatient: I... I guess so. It just... I can't really shake this feeling I have. Like... the bank, and sometimes my manager at work... they're just watching for me to mess up. Even when someone shows me it was just a normal paperwork thing, or a regular register error... I still feel it. So... yeah. I kind of believe it.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it difficult to organize your thoughts or express them clearly to others?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. I mean, I think I make sense when I talk. I haven't really noticed a problem with that.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the bank or your manager at work are actually plotting against you or is it just a feeling you have?\n\nPatient: I don't know if it's a plot. I just... like I said, I can't shake the feeling. Even when people try to show me it was just a normal paperwork thing, or a standard register error at the restaurant... I still feel like they're watching me. Waiting for me to mess up. So... I don't know. It feels real to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the whispering voice is coming from inside your mind or from an external source?\n\nPatient: I don't know... it sounds real. Like, when I'm closing up at the restaurant, or just sitting alone in my condo... I'll hear it. It's a low voice. It says my name, or whispers that I'm going to lose everything. It sounds like it's right there. I even turn around to check sometimes... but nobody's there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the bank or your manager at work are trying to harm you in any way?\n\nPatient: I don't know about harm... I just... like I said before, I can't shake the feeling that they're watching for me to mess up. The bank, my manager... even when people show me it was just a normal paperwork thing, or a register error... I just feel it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think someone is intentionally trying to harm you?\n\nPatient: No... I mean, like I just said, I don't think so. I haven't noticed anyone trying to actually hurt me. Just... what I told you before.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that events or conversations of others are specifically related to you?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, people talk about their own stuff. I don't... I don't think they're talking about me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that things you see or hear on TV, radio, or read in the newspaper are specifically about you?\n\nPatient: No. I just watch the news sometimes. It's just normal TV.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out.
